aw_compatch_milestone_1_classification_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { aawc_tribal_shatter_active = yes }
		error_log = "AAWC M1 classification begin"

		# Persist SH classification across post-start reclassification passes.
		# This prevents survivor pockets from being re-tagged as WH after game start.
		every_county = {
			limit = {
				has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker
				NOT = { has_variable = aawc_sh_lock }
			}
			set_variable = { name = aawc_sh_lock value = 1 }
			error_log = "AAWC M1 SH lock seeded from existing SH marker"
		}

		every_county = {
			remove_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker
			remove_county_modifier = aawc_wh_candidate_marker
			remove_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker
		}

		# RH pass
		every_county = {
			limit = { aawc_county_has_authored_landmark_candidate = yes }
			add_county_modifier = { modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker years = -1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_game_rule = aawc_age_of_silence }

			# Age of Silence has no survivor pockets. Authored landmarks remain RH;
			# every other county becomes WH.
			every_county = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }
					NOR = {
						this = title:c_unknown
						this = title:c_ruins
						this = title:c_wilderness
					}
				}
				add_county_modifier = { modifier = aawc_wh_candidate_marker years = -1 }
			}
			error_log = "AAWC M1 Age of Silence classified all non-RH counties as WH"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_game_rule = aawc_age_of_reclamation }
			# Age of Reclamation: RH first, then one Survivor Holding (SH) per de jure kingdom, then WH for all remaining valid counties.
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						title:c_hardhome = {
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker }
						}
						title:c_storrolds_point = {
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker }
						}
						title:c_brokenwood = {
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }

					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker }
						}
						title:c_the_bay_of_seals = {
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker }
						}
						title:c_woodvetch = {
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker }
						}

					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker }
						}
						title:c_gravewash = {

								add_county_modifier = { modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker years = -1 }

							}

						}

					}
					100 = {

						title:c_hypyris = {

							if = {

								limit = {					        NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }

					        NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker }




								}

								add_county_modifier = { modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker years = -1 }

							}

						}

					}
					100 = {

						title:c_nor_meran = {

							if = {

								limit = {					        NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }

					        NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker }




								}

								add_county_modifier = { modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker years = -1 }

							}

						}

					}
					100 = {

						title:c_ar_mynar = {

							if = {

								limit = {					        NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }

					        NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker }




								}

								add_county_modifier = { modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker years = -1 }

							}

						}

					}
				}
			}

			# Final WH pass: every remaining valid county that is neither RH nor SH becomes WH.
			every_county = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker }
					NOT = { has_variable = aawc_sh_lock }
					NOR = {
						this = title:c_unknown
						this = title:c_ruins
						this = title:c_wilderness
					}
				}
				add_county_modifier = { modifier = aawc_wh_candidate_marker years = -1 }
			}

			# SH lock enforcement: SH counties can never become WH/RH in post-start passes.
			every_county = {
				limit = { has_variable = aawc_sh_lock }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker } }
					add_county_modifier = { modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker years = -1 }
					error_log = "AAWC M1 SH lock restored missing SH marker"
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_county_modifier = aawc_wh_candidate_marker }
					remove_county_modifier = aawc_wh_candidate_marker
					error_log = "AAWC M1 SH lock removed stray WH marker"
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }
					remove_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker
					error_log = "AAWC M1 SH lock removed stray RH marker"
				}
			}

			# Lock any newly-selected SH counties so later reclassification cannot overwrite them.
			every_county = {
				limit = {
					has_county_modifier = aawc_survivor_pocket_marker
					NOT = { has_variable = aawc_sh_lock }
				}
				set_variable = { name = aawc_sh_lock value = 1 }
				error_log = "AAWC M1 SH lock seeded from newly-classified SH marker"
			}

		}
		else = {
			# Ruined World: adjacent counties have a 50% chance to become WH.
			every_county = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }
					any_neighboring_county = { has_county_modifier = aawc_rh_candidate_marker }
					NOR = {
						this = title:c_unknown
						this = title:c_ruins
						this = title:c_wilderness
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 50
					add_county_modifier = { modifier = aawc_wh_candidate_marker years = -1 }
				}
			}
		}
		aawc_debug_audit_sh_wh_integrity_effect = yes
		error_log = "AAWC M1 classification end"
	}
}
